Title: Nouvelle relation en forme de journal, d'un voyage fait en Égypte, par le P. Vansleb, R. D., en 1672 et 1673
Creator: Johann Michael Wansleben (title page: 'Par le P. Vansleb, R. D.')
Publisher: Paris: chez Estienne Michallet, rue S. Jacques (title page)
Publication Date: 1677 (title page: 'M. DC. LXXVII.', confirmed on the page image, leaf n6)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1677); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark
Language: fra
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_8n2JTTnOoMIC, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (578,494) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1677, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: readable French with the long s read as f; side notes are mixed into the lines. Google's stamp lines stand at OCR lines 1-19 and after the text. Word count of the host text: 92,156. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: One dated journal with no parts or chapters. Dedication to Cardinal d'Estrées at OCR lines 55-120; Vansleb's notice at 242-330; errata at about 320-360. The journal from 379 ('Estant à Marseille le 8 de May 1671') to 'FIN' at 17991. The account of the Copts in the opening description of Egypt at about 518-940; Coptic churches of Cairo at about 5536; the monasteries of St Macarius from 9437; Old Cairo at about 10400; St Antony's monastery at about 12997. Index at 18041-18830; privilege extract at 18837-18853.
